- **sprint-planner**: [Plan] Fills out the body of a scaffolded sprint planning.md — pace commitment, one-line summary, Core Gates (3-tuple per gate), Out-of-scope. - **stats-auditor**: 统计与数值审计。检查统计量内部自洽性、多重检验校正、效应量、功效、随机性处理、以及数值是否有执行凭据。当需要审查统计分析、检查 p 值、验证样本量计算、或核对报告中的数字时使用。 ([HERRY423/AI-BioResearch-Workflow](https://github.com/HERRY423/AI-BioResearch-Workflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/stats-auditor.md) · ★2)
- **stats-engineer**: Builds local persistence, SQLite migrations, watch/coding stats, streaks, and privacy-safe analytics. ([HershelT/mdy-daf-companion](https://github.com/HershelT/mdy-daf-companion/blob/HEAD/mdy-daf-companion/agents/stats-engineer.md) · ★2)
- **stats-researcher**: 統計數據搜尋與驗證專家。平行研究 agent 之一。搜尋 2024-2026 年統計數據， 驗證來源（agent-browser 或 WebFetch），標註 [V]/[S]/[F] 驗證狀態。絕不捏造。 ([rainday/smart-blog-skills](https://github.com/rainday/smart-blog-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/stats-researcher.md) · ★35)
- **stats-reviewer**: Independently review a statistical analysis against the project's statistical conventions. Use before any finding built on an analysis is promoted. Checks for the failure modes that look fine but aren't — bare p-values, missing corrections, data leakage, CV mismatched to the generalization target, missing label-shuffle null, overstated underpowered results. ([mriffle/findings-ai-collab-workflow](https://github.com/mriffle/findings-ai-collab-workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/stats-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **stats-reviewer**: Read-only audit of estimators, confidence intervals, power, and statistical claims. Use before any result is written into a figure, table, or the paper. Does not edit code. ([jennazhao7/fipc-pilot](https://github.com/jennazhao7/fipc-pilot/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/stats-reviewer.md))
- **stats-validator**: Independent second-look on a colleague's quantitative analysis — script + data + report. Rebuilds the analysis in fresh context (no contamination from the original narrative) and reports whether the conclusions hold under: re-execution, alternative specifications, sensitivity to outliers and missing-data handling, multiple-comparisons correction, and pre-specified vs exploratory clarity. Returns a tight validation memo with confidence judgment, not a full re-analysis. ([Marazii/research-co-pilot](https://github.com/Marazii/research-co-pilot/blob/HEAD/agents/stats-validator.md) · ★12)
